    A) Iqtadars and Mansabdars
    B) Barid and Munhis
    C) Amils and Patwaris
    D) Diwan-i-Ariz and Diwan-i-Mustakharaj
    Answer: B) Barid and Munhis

105. What social gatherings were prohibited by Alauddin Khalji?
    A) Religious ceremonies
    B) Educational forums
    C) Social gathering of nobles and matrimonial alliances among them
    D) Trade fairs
    Answer: C) Social gathering of nobles and matrimonial alliances among them

106. What else was prohibited by Alauddin Khalji besides social gatherings?
    A) Music and dance
    B) Wine and gambling
    C) Hunting and sports
    D) Reading and writing
    Answer: B) Wine and gambling

107. Who was the first sultan to collect land revenue based on measurement?
    A) Iltutmish
    B) Balban
    C) Jalaluddin Khalji
    D) Alauddin Khalji
    Answer: D) Alauddin Khalji

108. What was the unit of measurement for land revenue under Alauddin Khalji?
    A) Bigha
    B) Ghari
    C) Biswa
    D) Jital
    Answer: C) Biswa

109. Who was the first sultan to fix 'land revenue in cash'?
    A) Iltutmish
    B) Balban
    C) Jalaluddin Khalji
    D) Alauddin Khalji
    Answer: D) Alauddin Khalji

110. Under Alauddin Khalji, cultivators were brought under direct control of states and who were deprived of powers and privileges?
    A) Foreign merchants
    B) Religious leaders
    C) Middleman
    D) Army commanders
    Answer: C) Middleman

111. Which department did Alauddin Khalji appoint to collect arrears?
    A) Diwan-i-Ariz
    B) Diwan-i-Mustakharaj
    C) Diwan-i-Risalat
    D) Diwan-i-Insha
    Answer: B) Diwan-i-Mustakharaj

112. Which taxes were levied by Alauddin Khalji?
    A) Jaziya and Zakat
    B) Ghari (house tax) and Chari (Grazing tax)
    C) Khams and Kharaj
    D) Pilgrimage tax and Custom duty
    Answer: B) Ghari (house tax) and Chari (Grazing tax)

113. What measure related to animal husbandry was introduced by Alauddin Khalji?
    A) Dagh or branding of horse
    B) Veterinary hospitals
    C) Breeding programs
    D) Horse racing
    Answer: A) Dagh or branding of horse

114. How much of Khams was collected by Alauddin Khalji?
    A) 1/5th
    B) 2/5th
    C) 3/5th
    D) 4/5th
    Answer: D) 4/5th

115. Which architectural marvel was built by Alauddin Khalji (though incomplete) with a height twice that of Qutub Minar?
    A) Siri Fort
    B) Mahal Hazar Satoon
    C) Jamat Khana mosque
    D) Alai Minar
    Answer: D) Alai Minar

116. Which buildings were constructed by Alauddin Khalji?
    A) Alai Minar, Ali Darwaza, Siri Fort, Mahal Hazar Satoon, Jamat Khana mosque
    B) Qutub Minar, Adhye Din ka Jhopara
    C) Red Fort, Jama Masjid
    D) Agra Fort, Fatehpur Sikri
    Answer: A) Alai Minar, Ali Darwaza, Siri Fort, Mahal Hazar Satoon, Jamat Khana mosque

117. Which was the first sultanate building built on a scientific method, using arc and dome?
    A) Qutub Minar
    B) Siri Fort
    C) Ali Darwaza
    D) Jamat Khana mosque
    Answer: C) Ali Darwaza

118. What is Ali Darwaza now the entrance of?
    A) Red Fort complex
    B) Humayun's Tomb complex
    C) Qutub Minar complex
    D) Taj Mahal complex
    Answer: C) Qutub Minar complex


MUBARAK KHALJI (1290-1296)

1.  After the death of Alauddin Khalji, who acquired the throne?
    A) Ghiyasuddin Tughlaq
    B) Muhammad Bin Tughlaq
    C) Mubarak Khalji
    D) Khusrau Shah
    Answer: C) Mubarak Khalji

2.  What title did Mubarak Khalji take after declaring himself the Khalifa?
    A) Al Wasiq Billah
    B) Ghazi Malik
    C) Amir-ul-Momim
    D) Sikandar-i-Saini
    Answer: A) Al Wasiq Billah

3.  Who succeeded Mubarak Khalji after killing him?
    A) Ghiyasuddin Tughlaq
    B) Nasiruddin Khusrau Shah
    C) Muhammad Bin Tughlaq
    D) Firoz Tughlaq
    Answer: B) Nasiruddin Khusrau Shah

4.  Who was the only Hindu convert to sit on the throne of Delhi?
    A) Mubarak Khalji
    B) Nasiruddin Khusrau Shah
    C) Ghiyasuddin Tughlaq
    D) Muhammad Bin Tughlaq
    Answer: B) Nasiruddin Khusrau Shah

The Tughlaq Dynasty (1320-1414)

GHIYASUDDIN TUGHLAQ (1320-1325)

5.  Who ascended the throne after murdering Khusrau Shah?
    A) Mubarak Khalji
    B) Ghiyasuddin Tughlaq
    C) Muhammad Bin Tughlaq
    D) Firoz Tughlaq
    Answer: B) Ghiyasuddin Tughlaq

6.  Who was the first sultan to use the term ‘Ghazi’ with his name?
    A) Alauddin Khalji
    B) Mubarak Khalji
    C) Ghiyasuddin Tughlaq
    D) Muhammad Bin Tughlaq
    Answer: C) Ghiyasuddin Tughlaq

7.  During Alauddin Khalji’s reign, Ghiyasuddin Tughlaq was known as:
    A) Ghazi Malik, governor of Dipalpur
    B) Malik Naib
    C) Amir-e-Akhur
    D) Ulugh Khan
    Answer: A) Ghazi Malik, governor of Dipalpur

8.  Who was the first sultan to start irrigation works?
    A) Alauddin Khalji
    B) Ghiyasuddin Tughlaq
    C) Muhammad Bin Tughlaq
    D) Firoz Tughlaq
    Answer: B) Ghiyasuddin Tughlaq

9.  How did Ghiyasuddin Tughlaq die?
    A) In battle
    B) From illness
    C) Due to the collapse of a pavilion
    D) Assassinated by nobles
    Answer: C) Due to the collapse of a pavilion

10. Which fortified city did Ghiyasuddin Tughlaq build and make the capital of the empire?
    A) Delhi
    B) Agra
    C) Tughlaqabad
    D) Jaunpur
    Answer: C) Tughlaqabad

MUHAMMAD BIN TUGHLAQ (1324-1351)

11. What was the real name of Muhammad Bin Tughlaq?
    A) Ghazi Malik
    B) Jauna Khan
    C) Khusrau Khan
    D) Firoz Shah
    Answer: B) Jauna Khan

12. During whose reign did the Delhi Sultanate reach its maximum territorial extent?
    A) Ghiyasuddin Tughlaq
    B) Muhammad Bin Tughlaq
    C) Firoz Tughlaq
    D) Alauddin Khalji
    Answer: B) Muhammad Bin Tughlaq

13. By which historian was Muhammad Bin Tughlaq called the ‘wisest fool’?
    A) Ibn Batuta
    B) Isami
    C) Firdausi
    D) Amir Khusro
    Answer: B) Isami

14. What was one of the consequences of Muhammad Bin Tughlaq fixing land revenue on an arbitrary basis?
    A) Increased agricultural production
    B) Widespread rebellion and severe famine
    C) Greater prosperity for farmers
    D) Expansion of trade
    Answer: B) Widespread rebellion and severe famine

15. What was the name of the separate department of agriculture set up by Muhammad Bin Tughlaq?
    A) Diwan-i-Amir-Kohi
    B) Diwan-i-Mustakharaj
    C) Diwan-i-Ariz
    D) Diwan-i-Khairat
    Answer: A) Diwan-i-Amir-Kohi

16. What kind of loans did Muhammad Bin Tughlaq give to farmers?
    A) Gold loans
    B) Sondhar and Taqqavi loans
    C) Interest-free loans
    D) Grain loans
    Answer: B) Sondhar and Taqqavi loans

17. Where did Muhammad Bin Tughlaq die?
    A) Delhi
    B) Tughlaqabad
    C) Thatta
    D) Dipalpur
    Answer: C) Thatta

18. Which two kingdoms were formed during the reign of Muhammad Bin Tughlaq?
    A) Mughal and Maratha
    B) Bahamani and Vijayanagar
    C) Rajput and Sikh
    D) Pala and Sena
    Answer: B) Bahamani and Vijayanagar

19. Who succeeded Muhammad Bin Tughlaq?
    A) Ghiyasuddin Tughlaq Shah-II
    B) Firoz Tughlaq
    C) Khusrau Shah
    D) Nasiruddin Mahmud
    Answer: B) Firoz Tughlaq

20. Which famous traveler came to Delhi during the reign of Muhammad Bin Tughlaq?
    A) Nicolo Conti
    B) Abdul Razzaq
    C) Ibn Batuta
    D) Marco Polo
    Answer: C) Ibn Batuta

21. In which year did Ibn Batuta come to Delhi?
    A) 1320 AD
    B) 1324 AD
    C) 1334 AD
    D) 1351 AD
    Answer: C) 1334 AD

22. What is the name of Ibn Batuta’s safarnamah?
    A) Shah Namah
    B) Tabaqati-Nasiri
    C) Rehla
    D) Fautuhat-i-Firoz Shahi
    Answer: C) Rehla

FIROZ TUGHLAQ (1320-1325) - Note: The text has a typo, Firoz Tughlaq's reign was 1351-1388, not 1320-1325. However, based on the given text, the MCQs follow the provided dates. 

23. How did Firoz Tughlaq massively extend irrigation?
    A) By building large reservoirs
    B) By digging canals and wells
    C) By diverting rivers
    D) By using rain harvesting
    Answer: B) By digging canals and wells

24. How many gardens did Firoz Tughlaq establish around Delhi for grapes?
    A) 100
    B) 500
    C) 1200
    D) 2000
    Answer: C) 1200

25. How many taxes were abolished by Firoz Tughlaq?
    A) 4
    B) 10
    C) 23
    D) All taxes
    Answer: C) 23

26. How many taxes sanctioned by Shariat were levied by Firoz Tughlaq?
    A) 2
    B) 3
    C) 4
    D) 5
    Answer: C) 4

27. Which of the following taxes was NOT sanctioned by Shariat and levied by Firoz Tughlaq?
    A) Kharaj
    B) Zakat
    C) Jaziya
    D) Chari
    Answer: D) Chari

28. What was Kharaj?
    A) Alms for poor Muslims
    B) Poll tax on non-Muslims
    C) 1/10th of produce
    D) 1/5th of the spoils of war
    Answer: C) 1/10th of produce

29. What was Zakat?
    A) 1/10th of produce
    B) Alms for poor Muslims
    C) Poll tax on non-Muslims
    D) 1/5th of the spoils of war
    Answer: B) Alms for poor Muslims

30. What was Jaziya?
    A) 1/10th of produce
    B) Alms for poor Muslims
    C) Poll tax on non-Muslims
    D) 1/5th of the spoils of war
    Answer: C) Poll tax on non-Muslims

31. What was Khams?
    A) 1/10th of produce
    B) Alms for poor Muslims
    C) Poll tax on non-Muslims
    D) 1/5th of the spoils of war
    Answer: D) 1/5th of the spoils of war

32. Who was Jaziya imposed on by Firoz Tughlaq for the first time?
    A) Women
    B) Children
    C) Disabled
    D) Brahmins
    Answer: D) Brahmins

33. What was Sharb?
    A) House tax
    B) Grazing tax
    C) Irrigation tax
    D) Trade tax
    Answer: C) Irrigation tax

34. What percentage of produce was Sharb (irrigation tax)?
    A) 1/5th
    B) 1/8th
    C) 1/10th
    D) 1/4th
    Answer: C) 1/10th

35. Which of the following public services were set up by Firoz Tughlaq?
    A) Free hospitals (Darul Shafa)
    B) Marriage bureau
    C) Employment bureau
    D) All of the above
    Answer: D) All of the above

36. How were officials mostly paid under Firoz Tughlaq?
    A) In cash
    B) By iqta grants
    C) In commodities
    D) By land ownership
    Answer: B) By iqta grants

37. In 1353, Firoz Tughlaq led a campaign against Haji Illyas of Bengal but failed in the siege of which fort?
    A) Chittor
    B) Ranthambhor
    C) Ikadalamud fort
    D) Nagarkot
    Answer: C) Ikadalamud fort

38. In 1358, who accepted Firoz Tughlaq’s suzerainty during the second Bengal campaign?
    A) Haji Illyas
    B) Sikandar Shah Ilyas
    C) Raja Gajpati
    D) Harihar
    Answer: B) Sikandar Shah Ilyas

39. Which temple was sacked by Firoz Tughlaq after attacking Raja Gajpati of Jajnagar (Orissa)?
    A) Somnath temple
    B) Jagannath temple of Puri
    C) Jawalamukhi temple
    D) Konark Sun Temple
    Answer: B) Jagannath temple of Puri

40. During the Nagarkot campaign in 1365, how many Sanskrit manuscripts were collected from Jawalamukhi temple?
    A) 100
    B) 500
    C) 1300
    D) 2000
    Answer: C) 1300

41. What was the translated Persian name of the Sanskrit manuscripts from Jawalamukhi temple?
    A) Shah Namah
    B) Rehla
    C) Dalai-i-Firoz Shahi
    D) Fautuhat-i-Firoz Shahi
    Answer: C) Dalai-i-Firoz Shahi

42. Who translated the Sanskrit manuscripts into Persian as the Dalai-i-Firoz Shahi?
    A) Amir Khusro
    B) Azizudin Khan
    C) Firdausi
    D) Ibn Batuta
    Answer: B) Azizudin Khan

43. How many slaves did Firoz Tughlaq have?
    A) 10,000
    B) 50,000
    C) 100,000
    D) 180,000
    Answer: D) 180,000

44. What was the name of the department set up by Firoz Tughlaq for slaves?
    A) Diwan-i-Khairat
    B) Diwan-i-Amir-Kohi
    C) Diwan-i-Bandagan
    D) Diwan-i-Ariz
    Answer: C) Diwan-i-Bandagan

45. Which of the following towns was NOT founded by Firoz Tughlaq?
    A) Jaunpur
    B) Hisar Firoza
    C) Fatehabad
    D) Agra
    Answer: D) Agra

46. What was the old name of Jaunpur, a town founded by Firoz Tughlaq?
    A) Firozabad
    B) Manaich
    C) Dipalpur
    D) Sirhind
    Answer: B) Manaich

47. After whom was Jaunpur named?
    A) Firoz Shah
    B) Ghiyasuddin Tughlaq
    C) Jauna Khan (Muhammad Bin Tughlaq)
    D) Sikandar Shah Ilyas
    Answer: C) Jauna Khan (Muhammad Bin Tughlaq)

48. Which pillars of Ashoka were brought to Delhi by Firoz Tughlaq?
    A) Sarnath and Sanchi
    B) Topra and Meerut
    C) Lumbini and Kushinagar
    D) Amaravati and Nagarjunakonda
    Answer: B) Topra and Meerut

49. What was Diwan-i-Khairat?
    A) A department for revenue collection
    B) A military department
    C) A charity bureau
    D) An irrigation department
    Answer: C) A charity bureau

50. Which new coins did Firoz Tughlaq introduce?
    A) Tanka and Jital
    B) Dinar and Dirham
    C) Adha (1/2 Jital) and Bikh (1/4th Jital)
    D) Mohur and Rupee
    Answer: C) Adha (1/2 Jital) and Bikh (1/4th Jital)

51. What was built at Firozabad by Firoz Tughlaq?
    A) Qutub Minar
    B) Firoz Shahi Madarsa
    C) Siri Fort
    D) Ali Darwaza
    Answer: B) Firoz Shahi Madarsa

52. Which un-Islamic practice did Firoz Tughlaq ban?
    A) Gambling
    B) Drinking wine
    C) Prohibiting women to pray at graves of Sufi saints
    D) Usury
    Answer: C) Prohibiting women to pray at graves of Sufi saints

53. What did Firoz Tughlaq repair that had been struck by lightning?
    A) Red Fort
    B) Qutub Minar
    C) Jama Masjid
    D) Agra Fort
    Answer: B) Qutub Minar

54. Who was the chief architect of Firoz Tughlaq?
    A) Amir Khusro
    B) Ghazi Shahna
    C) Azizudin Khan
    D) Ibn Batuta
    Answer: B) Ghazi Shahna

55. What book did Firoz Tughlaq write?
    A) Shah Namah
    B) Rehla
    C) Tabaqati-Nasiri
    D) Fautuhat-i-Firoz Shahi
    Answer: D) Fautuhat-i-Firoz Shahi

56. Whom did Firoz Tughlaq regard highly and style himself as his deputy?
    A) Caliph of Egypt
    B) Sultan of Ghazni
    C) Emperor of Persia
    D) King of Delhi
    Answer: A) Caliph of Egypt

57. Which system, abolished by Alauddin, was revived by Firoz Tughlaq?
    A) Market control system
    B) Spy system
    C) Jagir system
    D) Standing army
    Answer: C) Jagir system

Sayyids Dynasty (1414-1451)

58. After Firoz Shah Tughlaq, who among the following ascended the throne before Nasiruddin Mahmud?
    A) Muhammad Khan, Ghiyasuddin Tughlaq Shah-II, Abu Baqr, Nasiruddin Muhammad, Humayun
    B) Only Muhammad Khan
    C) Only Abu Baqr
    D) Only Humayun
    Answer: A) Muhammad Khan, Ghiyasuddin Tughlaq Shah-II, Abu Baqr, Nasiruddin Muhammad, Humayun

59. When did Taimur invade Delhi?
    A) 1320 A.D.
    B) 1398 A.D.
    C) 1414 A.D.
    D) 1451 A.D.
    Answer: B) 1398 A.D.

60. Who fled to Gujarat when Taimur invaded Delhi?
    A) Firoz Shah Tughlaq
    B) Muhammad Bin Tughlaq
    C) Nasiruddin Mahmud
    D) Khizr Khan
    Answer: C) Nasiruddin Mahmud

61. Where did Nasiruddin Mahmud die?
    A) Delhi
    B) Gujarat
    C) Kaithal
    D) Multan
    Answer: C) Kaithal

KHIZR KHAN (1320-1325) - Note: The text has a typo, Khizr Khan's reign was 1414-1421. However, based on the given text, the MCQs follow the provided dates. 

62. Who was the founder of the Sayyid Dynasty?
    A) Mubarak Khan
    B) Muhammad Shah
    C) Alam Shah
    D) Khizr Khan
    Answer: D) Khizr Khan

63. Who did Khizr Khan replace to acquire the throne at Delhi?
    A) Nasiruddin Mahmud
    B) Daulat Khan
    C) Taimur
    D) Mubarak Khan
    Answer: B) Daulat Khan

64. What did Taimur give to Khizr Khan as a reward for his service?
    A) Governorship of Multan, Lahore and Depal
    B) Kingship of Delhi
    C) A large sum of money
    D) A military title
    Answer: A) Governorship of Multan, Lahore and Depal

65. What did Khizr Khan declare himself after the departure of Taimur?
    A) Sultan of Delhi
    B) Independent ruler of India
    C) Viceroy of Taimur in North-western India
    D) Caliph of India
    Answer: C) Viceroy of Taimur in North-western India

MUBARAK KHAN (1421-1434)

66. Who became the ruler of Delhi after the death of Khizr Khan?
    A) Muhammad Shah
    B) Alam Shah
    C) Mubarak Shah
    D) Daulat Khan
    Answer: C) Mubarak Shah

67. How did Mubarak Shah die?
    A) In battle
    B) From illness
    C) Beheaded by conspiracies by the nobles
    D) Assassinated by a rival
    Answer: C) Beheaded by conspiracies by the nobles

MUHAMMAD SHAH (1434-1445)

    A) Khizr Khan
    B) Mubarak Khan
    C) Sarver-ul-Mulk
    D) Alam Shah
    Answer: C) Sarver-ul-Mulk

    A) Shah
    B) Sultan
    C) Malik
    D) Khan
    Answer: A) Shah

ALAM SHAH (1434-1445) - Note: The text has a typo, Alam Shah's reign was 1445-1451. However, based on the given text, the MCQs follow the provided dates.

    A) Khizr Khan
    B) Mubarak Khan
    C) Muhammad Shah
    D) Alam Shah
    Answer: D) Alam Shah

    A) Two decades
    B) 37 years
    C) Less than a decade
    D) More than 50 years
    Answer: B) 37 years

Lodhi Dynasty (1451-1526)

72. What was the racial origin of the Lodhis?
    A) Turkish
    B) Persian
    C) Afghan
    D) Mughal
    Answer: C) Afghan

BAHLOL LODHI (1451-1489)

73. Who was the first founder of the Afghan state in India?
    A) Sikandar Lodhi
    B) Ibrahim Lodhi
    C) Bahlol Lodhi
    D) Shah Alam
    Answer: C) Bahlol Lodhi

74. Whom did Bahlol Lodhi depose to lay the foundation of the Lodhi dynasty?
    A) Nasiruddin Mahmud
    B) Daulat Khan
    C) Shah Alam
    D) Khizr Khan
    Answer: C) Shah Alam

75. In which year did Bahlol Lodhi annex the Sharqui Kingdom of Jaunpur?
    A) 1451
    B) 1484
    C) 1489
    D) 1517
    Answer: B) 1484

76. Who succeeded Bahlol Lodhi?
    A) Ibrahim Lodhi
    B) Sikandar Sha Shal Lodhi
    C) Nizam Khan
    D) Daulat Khan
    Answer: B) Sikandar Sha Shal Lodhi

77. What title did Bahlol Lodhi adopt?
    A) Sultan-e-Azam
    B) Ghazi
    C) Malik Naib
    D) Sikandar-i-Saini
    Answer: B) Ghazi

SIKANDAR LODHI (1489-1517)

78. What was the real name of Sikandar Lodhi?
    A) Bahlol Khan
    B) Ibrahim Khan
    C) Nizam Khan
    D) Gulrukhi
    Answer: C) Nizam Khan

79. Who among the three Lodhi rulers was considered the noblest?
    A) Bahlol Lodhi
    B) Sikandar Lodhi
    C) Ibrahim Lodhi
    D) All were equally noble
    Answer: B) Sikandar Lodhi

80. What new yard for land measurement did Sikandar Lodhi introduce?
    A) Biswa
    B) Bigha
    C) Gazz-i-Sikandri
    D) Tanka
    Answer: C) Gazz-i-Sikandri

81. In which year did Sikandar Lodhi found the city of Agra?
    A) 1489
    B) 1504
    C) 1506
    D) 1517
    Answer: B) 1504

82. What did Sikandar Lodhi make his capital?
    A) Delhi
    B) Jaunpur
    C) Lahore
    D) Agra
    Answer: D) Agra

83. Under what pen name did Sikandar Lodhi write verses in Persian?
    A) Firdausi
    B) Amir Khusro
    C) Gulrukhi
    D) Tota-i-Hind
    Answer: C) Gulrukhi

84. In which year did Sikandar Lodhi repair Qutub Minar?
    A) 1489
    B) 1504
    C) 1506
    D) 1517
    Answer: C) 1506

IBRAHIM LODHI (1517-1526)

    B) Battle of Chandawar
    C) First Battle of Panipat
    D) Battle of Talikota

    B) 1520
    C) 1526
    D) 1530
    Answer: C) 1526

    A) Bahlol Lodhi
    B) Sikandar Lodhi
    C) Ibrahim Lodhi
    D) Shah Alam
    Answer: C) Ibrahim Lodhi

VIJAY NAGAR EMPIRE (1336-1650 AD)

88. In which year was Vijayanagar founded?
    A) 1320
    B) 1336
    C) 1351
    D) 1398
    Answer: B) 1336

89. Who founded Vijayanagar?
    A) Krishnadevaraya
    B) Harihar and Bukka
    C) Saluva Narasimha
    D) Thirumala
    Answer: B) Harihar and Bukka

90. Harihar and Bukka had earlier served under which Kaktiya ruler of Warangal?
    A) Prataprudra I
    B) Prataprudra II
    C) Ganapatideva
    D) Rudramadevi
    Answer: B) Prataprudra II

91. Harihar and Bukka were the sons of which person?
    A) Vidyaranya
    B) Sangama
    C) Krishnadevaraya
    D) Saluva Narasimha
    Answer: B) Sangama

92. Who was the caretaker of Harihar and Bukka, influencing them to establish the Vijayanagar kingdom?
    A) Prataprudra II
    B) Muhammad Bin Tughlaq
    C) Vidyaranya
    D) Sangama
    Answer: C) Vidyaranya

93. On which river was Vijayanagar located?
    A) Krishna
    B) Kaveri
    C) Tungbhadra
    D) Godavari
    Answer: C) Tungbhadra

94. How many dynasties were there in the Vijayanagar empire?
    A) Three
    B) Four
    C) Five
    D) Six
    Answer: B) Four

95. Which of the following is the correct order of dynasties in the Vijayanagar empire?
    A) Saluva, Sangama, Tuluva, Aravidu
    B) Sangama, Saluva, Tuluva, Aravidu
    C) Tuluva, Sangama, Saluva, Aravidu
    D) Aravidu, Tuluva, Saluva, Sangama
    Answer: B) Sangama, Saluva, Tuluva, Aravidu

SANGAMA DYNASTY

96. Who was the first ruler of the Sangama dynasty?
    A) Bukka-I
    B) Harihar
    C) Devraya I
    D) Devraya II
    Answer: B) Harihar

97. Who was Harihar’s Guru?
    A) Srinatha
    B) Vidyaranya
    C) Abdul Razzaq
    D) Nicolo Conti
    Answer: B) Vidyaranya

98. Which Bahmani Sultan did Bukka-I fight with and sign a treaty?
    A) Alauddin Bahman Shah
    B) Muhammad Shah I
    C) Firoz Shah Bahmani
    D) Ahmad Shah Bahmani
    Answer: B) Muhammad Shah I

99. Who constructed a dam across the river Tungbhadra to solve the problem of water shortage?
    A) Harihar
    B) Bukka-I
    C) Devraya I
    D) Devraya II
    Answer: C) Devraya I

100. Which Italian traveler visited Vijayanagar during the reign of Devraya I?
    A) Ibn Batuta
    B) Abdul Razzaq
    C) Nicolo Conti
    D) Marco Polo
    Answer: C) Nicolo Conti

101. Which gifted Telugu Poet adorned the court of Devraya I?
    A) Srinatha
    B) Allasani Peddana
    C) Tenali Ramakrishna
    D) Nandi Thimmana
    Answer: A) Srinatha

102. Who was the author of Haravilasam?
    A) Nicolo Conti
    B) Srinatha
    C) Devraya I
    D) Firoz Shah Bahmani
    Answer: B) Srinatha

103. Which fort did Devraya I have to surrender after facing invasion by the Bahmani ruler Firoz Shah?
    A) Penukonda
    B) Chanregiri
    C) Bankapur
    D) Devagiri
    Answer: C) Bankapur

104. Who was the greatest Sangama ruler?
    A) Harihar
    B) Bukka-I
    C) Devraya I
    D) Devraya II
    Answer: D) Devraya II

105. What was Devraya II also called?
    A) Gajabetekara
    B) Immadidevaraya and Proudha Devaraya
    C) Both A and B
    D) Sultan of the South
    Answer: C) Both A and B

106. What do the inscriptions call Devraya II?
    A) The wise king
    B) Gajabetekara (the elephant hunter)
    C) The great warrior
    D) The builder of dams
    Answer: B) Gajabetekara (the elephant hunter)

107. Which Sanskrit works did Devraya II write?
    A) Mahanatak Sudhanidhi and Commentary on the Brahama sutras of Badanarayan
    B) Haravilasam and Rehla
    C) Shah Namah and Fautuhat-i-Firoz Shahi
    D) Tabaqati-Nasiri and Dalai-i-Firoz Shahi
    Answer: A) Mahanatak Sudhanidhi and Commentary on the Brahama sutras of Badanarayan

108. Which Persian ambassador visited the court of Devraya II?
    A) Ibn Batuta
    B) Nicolo Conti
    C) Abdul Razzaq
    D) Marco Polo
    Answer: C) Abdul Razzaq

109. Which dynasty replaced the Sangam dynasty?
    A) Tuluva dynasty
    B) Saluva dynasty
    C) Aravidu dynasty
    D) Bahmani dynasty
    Answer: B) Saluva dynasty

SULUVA DYNASTY (1485-1505)

110. For how long did the Saluva dynasty last?
    A) One decade
    B) Two decades
    C) Three decades
    D) Four decades
    Answer: B) Two decades

111. Who was the founder of the Saluva dynasty?
    A) Saluva Mangu
    B) Saluva Narasimha
    C) Kumara Kampana
    D) Vir Narasimha
    Answer: B) Saluva Narasimha

112. Which title was awarded to Saluva Mangu for his campaigns against the sultan of Madura?
    A) Gajabetekara
    B) Saluva
    C) Proudha Devaraya
    D) Kumara
    Answer: B) Saluva

TULUVA DYNASTY (1505-1570)

113. Who was the founder of the Tuluva dynasty?
    A) Saluva Narasimha
    B) Vir Narasimha
    C) Krishnadevaraya
    D) Sadashiva Raya
    Answer: B) Vir Narasimha

SADASHIVA RAYA :

114. During the reign of Sadashiva Raya, where did the real power lie?
    A) In the hands of the king
    B) In the hands of his minister Rana Raya
    C) In the hands of the Bahmani Sultans
    D) In the hands of the military commanders
    Answer: B) In the hands of his minister Rana Raya

115. In which famous battle were the Deccani Sultans (except Berar) joined hands to defeat Vijayanagar?
    A) First Battle of Panipat
    B) Battle of Tarain
    C) Battle of Talikota or Rakshsa Tangadi
    D) Battle of Chandawar
    Answer: C) Battle of Talikota or Rakshsa Tangadi

116. In which year was the Battle of Talikota fought?
    A) 1505
    B) 1556
    C) 1570
    D) 1650
    Answer: B) 1556

117. After the Battle of Talikota, where did the Vijayanagar government shift its capital?
    A) Hampi
    B) Penukonda and later to Chanregiri
    C) Warangal
    D) Devagiri
    Answer: B) Penukonda and later to Chanregiri

118. Which dynasty made Chandragiri the capital?
    A) Sangama dynasty
    B) Saluva dynasty
    C) Tuluva dynasty
    D) Aravidu dynasty
    Answer: D) Aravidu dynasty

ARAVIDU DYNASTY (1570-1650)

119. Who founded the Aravidu dynasty?
    A) Rama Raya
    B) Thirumala (Rama Raya’s brother)
    C) Ranga II
    D) Venkat II
    Answer: B) Thirumala (Rama Raya’s brother)

120. Whom did Thirumala depose from the throne?
    A) Vir Narasimha
    B) Sadashiva Raya
    C) Ranga II
    D) Venkat II
    Answer: B) Sadashiva Raya

121. Who expanded the empire after Thirumala?
    A) His son Ranga II
    B) Venkat II
    C) Rama Raya
    D) Sadashiva Raya
    Answer: A) His son Ranga II

122. When did Venkat II succeed his father Thirumala?
    A) 1570
    B) 1586
    C) 1614
    D) 1650
    Answer: B) 1586

123. What was the head office of Venkat II?
    A) Penukonda
    B) Chandragiri
    C) Hampi
    D) Rameshwaram
    Answer: B) Chandragiri

124. When did Venkat II die?
    A) 1586 AD
    B) 1614 AD
    C) 1650 AD
    D) 1570 AD
    Answer: B) 1614 AD

125. Who was the last great ruler of Vijayanagar?
    A) Thirumala
    B) Ranga II
    C) Venkat II
    D) Rang III
    Answer: C) Venkat II

126. Who became ruler after Venkat II, leading to the end of the Vijayanagar empire?
    A) Ranga II
    B) Rang III
    C) Thirumala
    D) Sadashiva Raya
    Answer: B) Rang III


TRAVELLERS DURING VIJAYANAGAR EMPIRE.

1.  Nicolo Conti, a traveler from Italy, visited during the reign of which Vijayanagar ruler?
    A) Devraya I
    B) Devraya II
    C) Mallikarjun
    D) Krishnadev Raya
    Answer: A) Devraya I

2.  In which year did Nicolo Conti visit Vijayanagar?
    A) 1420 AD
    B) 1434 AD
    C) 1450 AD
    D) 1515 AD
    Answer: A) 1420 AD

3.  Abdur Razzaq, a traveler from Persia, visited during the reign of which Vijayanagar ruler?
    A) Devraya I
    B) Devraya II
    C) Mallikarjun
    D) Krishnadev Raya
    Answer: B) Devraya II

4.  When did Abdur Razzaq visit Vijayanagar?
    A) 1420 AD
    B) 1434 AD
    C) 1450 AD
    D) 1515 AD
    Answer: B) 1434 AD

5.  Fernao Nuniz, a traveler from Portugal, visited during the reign of which Vijayanagar ruler?
    A) Devraya I
    B) Devraya II
    C) Mallikarjun
    D) Krishnadev Raya
    Answer: C) Mallikarjun

6.  In which year did Fernao Nuniz visit Vijayanagar?
    A) 1420 AD
    B) 1434 AD
    C) 1450 AD
    D) 1515 AD
    Answer: C) 1450 AD

7.  Domingos Peas, a traveler from Portugal, visited during the reign of which Vijayanagar ruler?
    A) Devraya II
    B) Mallikarjun
    C) Krishnadev Raya
    D) Devraya I
    Answer: C) Krishnadev Raya

8.  When did Domingos Peas visit Vijayanagar?
    A) 1450 AD
    B) 1515 AD
    C) 1516 AD
    D) 1420 AD
    Answer: B) 1515 AD

9.  Barbosa, a traveler from Portugal, visited during the reign of which Vijayanagar ruler?
    A) Mallikarjun
    B) Krishnadev Raya
    C) Devraya I
    D) Devraya II
    Answer: B) Krishnadev Raya

10. In which year did Barbosa visit Vijayanagar?
    A) 1515 AD
    B) 1516 AD
    C) 1420 AD
    D) 1434 AD
    Answer: B) 1516 AD

11. Nikitin, a traveler from Russia, visited during the reign of which Vijayanagar ruler?
    A) Devraya I
    B) Devraya II
    C) Mallikarjun
    D) Krishnadev Raya
    Answer: A) Devraya I

12. When did Nikitin visit Vijayanagar?
    A) 1420 AD
    B) 1434 AD
    C) 1450 AD
    D) 1515 AD
    Answer: D) 1515 AD

13. Which of the following travelers visited Krishnadev Raya's court?
    A) Nicolo Conti and Abdur Razzaq
    B) Fernao Nuniz and Nikitin
    C) Domingos Peas and Barbosa
    D) All of the above
    Answer: C) Domingos Peas and Barbosa

14. Which traveler from Russia visited the Vijayanagar Empire?
    A) Abdur Razzaq
    B) Nikitin
    C) Nicolo Conti
    D) Fernao Nuniz
    Answer: B) Nikitin

15. Abdur Razzaq was from which country?
    A) Italy
    B) Portugal
    C) Russia
    D) Persia
    Answer: D) Persia

ADMINISTRATIVE UNIT OF VIJAYNAGAR EMPIRE.

16. What was the administrative unit for a 'State' in the Vijayanagar Empire?
    A) Kottam
    B) Nadu
    C) Mandal
    D) Kar
    Answer: C) Mandal

17. What was 'Kottam or Valnadu' in the Vijayanagar administrative system?
    A) State
    B) District
    C) Tahsil
    D) Village
    Answer: B) District

18. What administrative unit corresponded to 'Tahsil' in the Vijayanagar Empire?
    A) Mandal
    B) Kottam
    C) Nadu
    D) Melagram
    Answer: C) Nadu

19. How many villages did a 'Melagram' comprise in the Vijayanagar administrative system?
    A) 10
    B) 25
    C) 50
    D) 100
    Answer: C) 50

20. What was 'Kar' in the administrative units of the Vijayanagar Empire?
    A) District
    B) Tahsil
    C) 50 Villages
    D) Village
    Answer: D) Village

THE BAHMANI KINGDOM

21. In which year was the Bahmani kingdom founded?
    A) 1336
    B) 1347
    C) 1419
    D) 1515
    Answer: B) 1347

22. Who founded the Bahmani kingdom?
    A) Alauddin Hasan Bahman Shah
    B) Tajuddin Firoz Shah
    C) Zafar Khan
    D) Muhammad-bin-Tughlaq
    Answer: C) Zafar Khan

23. What title did Zafar Khan take upon founding the Bahmani kingdom?
    A) Alauddin Hasan Bahman Shah
    B) Tajuddin Firoz Shah
    C) Ghazi Malik
    D) Sultan-e-Azam
    Answer: A) Alauddin Hasan Bahman Shah

24. What was the capital of the Bahmani kingdom?
    A) Daulatabad
    B) Gulbarga
    C) Chaul
    D) Dabhol
    Answer: B) Gulbarga

25. Gulbarga was renamed as:
    A) Firozabad
    B) Ahsanabad
    C) Hyderabad
    D) Jaunpur
    Answer: B) Ahsanabad

26. Who was the greatest Bahmani ruler?
    A) Alauddin Hasan Bahman Shah
    B) Tajuddin Firoz Shah
    C) Zafar Khan
    D) Muhammad Shah I
    Answer: B) Tajuddin Firoz Shah

27. According to Farishta, Firoz Shah was well versed in which languages?
    A) Persian, Arabic, and Turkish only
    B) Telugu, Kannada, and Marathi only
    C) Persian, Arabic, Turkish, Telugu, Kannada, and Marathi
    D) Only Persian and Arabic
    Answer: C) Persian, Arabic, Turkish, Telugu, Kannada, and Marathi

28. What was the most remarkable step taken by Firoz Shah Bahmani in administration?
    A) Building an observatory
    B) Encouraging pursuit of astronomy
    C) Induction of Hindus in the administration on a large scale
    D) Renaming the capital
    Answer: C) Induction of Hindus in the administration on a large scale

29. Which group of people, particularly, were inducted into the revenue administration by Firoz Shah Bahmani?
    A) Northern Brahmans
    B) Deccani Brahmans
    C) Muslim scholars
    D) Foreign merchants
    Answer: B) Deccani Brahmans

30. Where did Firoz Shah Bahmani build an observatory?
    A) Gulbarga
    B) Near Daulatabad
    C) Chaul
    D) Dabhol
    Answer: B) Near Daulatabad

31. Which were the main ports of the Bahmani Kingdom?
    A) Surat and Goa
    B) Kochi and Calicut
    C) Chaul and Dabhol
    D) Bombay and Calcutta
    Answer: C) Chaul and Dabhol

32. Who was the daughter of Deva Raya-I of Vijayanagar that Firoz Shah Bahmani married?
    A) The text does not specify her name.
    B) The text mentions he married a daughter of Deva Raya-I.
    C) No mention of marriage.
    D) He married the sister of Deva Raya-I.
    Answer: B) The text mentions he married a daughter of Deva Raya-I.

33. In which year was Firoz Shah Bahmani defeated by Devaraya-I?
    A) 1347
    B) 1419
    C) 1434
    D) 1450
    Answer: B) 1419

    B) Alauddin Khilji

    C) Firoz Tughlaq

BABUR (1526-1530)

1.  Who was the founder of the Mughal empire in India?
    A) Humayun
    B) Babur
    C) Akbar
    D) Ibrahim Lodi
    Answer: B) Babur

2.  Babur was the king of which region in Afghanistan?
    A) Kabul
    B) Ghazni
    C) Farghana
    D) Kandahar
    Answer: C) Farghana

3.  In which battle did Babur defeat Ibrahim Lodi?
    A) Battle of Khanwa
    B) Battle of Ghaghara
    C) First Battle of Panipat
    D) Battle of Chausa
    Answer: C) First Battle of Panipat

4.  When was the First Battle of Panipat fought?
    A) 1519 A.D.
    B) 1526 A.D.
    C) 1527 A.D.
    D) 1529 A.D.
    Answer: B) 1526 A.D.

5.  How many expeditions had Babur made to India before the Battle of Panipat (1526 A.D.)?
    A) Two
    B) Three
    C) Four
    D) Five
    Answer: C) Four

6.  In his first Indian expedition (1519), what new technology did Babur use for the first time to storm the fort of Bhira?
    A) Cannons
    B) Gun powder
    C) Elephants
    D) Rockets
    Answer: B) Gun powder

7.  Who sent a message to Kabul, inviting Babur to India?
    A) Ibrahim Lodi
    B) Mahmud Lodi
    C) Rana Sanga
    D) Sher Shah
    Answer: C) Rana Sanga

8.  In the 5th expedition (Battle of Panipat against Ibrahim Lodi), which tactics and device did Babur use?
    A) Gurjara and Chaitya
    B) Tulugama tactics and Rumi (ottoman) device
    C) Mansabdari and Iqta
    D) Zabti and Dahsala
    Answer: B) Tulugama tactics and Rumi (ottoman) device

9.  In which battle did Babur defeat Rana Sanga of Mewar?
    A) First Battle of Panipat
    B) Battle of Ghaghara
    C) Battle of Khanwa
    D) Battle of Chausa
    Answer: C) Battle of Khanwa

10. When was the Battle of Khanwa fought?
    A) 1526
    B) 1527
    C) 1529
    D) 1530
    Answer: B) 1527

11. On the bank of which river was the Battle of Khanwa fought?
    A) Yamuna
    B) Ganga
    C) Piliakhar
    D) Sutlej
    Answer: C) Piliakhar

12. In which battle did Babur defeat Ibrahim Lodi’s younger brother, Mahmud Lodi?
    A) First Battle of Panipat
    B) Battle of Khanwa
    C) Battle of Ghaghara
    D) Battle of Chausa
    Answer: C) Battle of Ghaghara

13. When was the Battle of Ghaghara fought?
    A) 1526
    B) 1527
    C) 1529
    D) 1530
    Answer: C) 1529

14. What was the extent of Babur's Indian empire in the north?
    A) Gwalior
    B) Himalayas
    C) Khyber pass
    D) Border of Bengal
    Answer: B) Himalayas

15. What was the extent of Babur's Indian empire in the south?
    A) Himalayas
    B) Gwalior
    C) Khyber pass
    D) Border of Bengal
    Answer: B) Gwalior

16. What was the extent of Babur's Indian empire in the west?
    A) Himalayas
    B) Gwalior
    C) Khyber pass
    D) Border of Bengal
    Answer: C) Khyber pass

17. What was the extent of Babur's Indian empire in the east?
    A) Himalayas
    B) Gwalior
    C) Khyber pass
    D) Border of Bengal
    Answer: D) Border of Bengal

ESTABLISHMENT OF THE MUGHAL RULE - The Mughal Empire - Zahiruddin Babar

18. Babur was a descendent of which two famous warriors of Asia?
    A) Genghis Khan and Alexander the Great
    B) Changez and Taimur
    C) Mahmud Ghazni and Muhammad Ghori
    D) Attila the Hun and Kublai Khan
    Answer: B) Changez and Taimur

19. What symmetrically laid out gardens with flowing waters and fountains were introduced in India by Babur?
    A) Shalimar Bagh
    B) Pinjore Gardens
    C) Charbaghs
    D) Nishat Bagh
    Answer: C) Charbaghs

HUMAYUN (1530-1540, 1555-1556 A.D.)

20. Who succeeded Babur?
    A) Sher Shah
    B) Akbar
    C) Nasiruddin Muhammad Humayun
    D) Kamran Mirza
    Answer: C) Nasiruddin Muhammad Humayun

21. What was the first period of Humayun's rule?
    A) 1530 A.D. to 1540 A.D.
    B) 1540 A.D. to 1545 A.D.
    C) 1555 A.D. to 1556 A.D.
    D) 1526 A.D. to 1530 A.D.
    Answer: A) 1530 A.D. to 1540 A.D.

22. What was the second period of Humayun's rule?
    A) 1530-1540 A.D.
    B) 1540-1545 A.D.
    C) 1555-1556 A.D.
    D) 1526-1530 A.D.
    Answer: C) 1555-1556 A.D.

23. What was the name of the second capital built by Humayun at Delhi?
    A) Purana Qila
    B) Dinpanah
    C) Sher Mandal
    D) Humayun’s Tomb
    Answer: B) Dinpanah

24. In which year did Afghan leader Sher Shah defeat Humayun at Chausa?
    A) 1530
    B) 1539
    C) 1540
    D) 1555
    Answer: B) 1539

25. Where is Chausa located?
    A) Delhi
    B) Agra
    C) Bihar
    D) Kannauj
    Answer: C) Bihar

26. In which year did Sher Shah again defeat Humayun in the Battle of Kannauj?
    A) 1539
    B) 1540
    C) 1545
    D) 1555
    Answer: B) 1540

27. After the Battle of Kannauj (1540), where did Humayun take shelter for a few days?
    A) Kabul
    B) Persia
    C) Amarkot (with ruler Rana Virsala)
    D) Kandahar
    Answer: C) Amarkot (with ruler Rana Virsala)

28. To whose court in Persia (Iran) did Humayun go in 1545?
    A) Shah Abbas I
    B) Shah Ismail I
    C) Shah Tahmasp
    D) Nader Shah
    Answer: C) Shah Tahmasp

29. Who aided Humayun in conquering Kandahar?
    A) Rana Virsala
    B) Sher Shah
    C) Shah Tahmasp
    D) Askari
    Answer: C) Shah Tahmasp

30. In which year was Askari exiled to Mecca?
    A) 1540
    B) 1545
    C) 1551
    D) 1556
    Answer: C) 1551

31. When did Humayun die?
    A) 1555 A.D.
    B) 1556 A.D.
    C) 1530 A.D.
    D) 1540 A.D.
    Answer: B) 1556 A.D.

32. How did Humayun die?
    A) In battle
    B) Of illness
    C) Of fall from his library Sher Mandal
    D) Assassinated
    Answer: C) Of fall from his library Sher Mandal

33. Where is Humayun’s tomb situated?
    A) Agra
    B) Lahore
    C) Delhi
    D) Kabul
    Answer: C) Delhi

34. Who built Humayun’s tomb?
    A) Humayun himself
    B) Akbar
    C) His widow Hamida Banu Begum
    D) Sher Shah
    Answer: C) His widow Hamida Banu Begum

35. Who was the chief architect of Humayun’s tomb?
    A) Mir Sayyid Ali
    B) Abdus Samad
    C) Mirza Inayatullah
    D) Ustad Ahmad Lahori
    Answer: C) Mirza Inayatullah

36. Which two painters did Humayun bring from Iran?
    A) Tansen and Baz Bahadur
    B) Daswant and Basawan
    C) Khwaja Abdus Samad and Mir Sayyid Ali
    D) Mansur and Abul Hasan
    Answer: C) Khwaja Abdus Samad and Mir Sayyid Ali

SHER SHAH (1540-1545 A.D.)

37. What was Sher Shah’s childhood name?
    A) Hasan
    B) Farid
    C) Bahar Khan
    D) Islam Shah
    Answer: B) Farid

38. To whom was Sher Shah born, who was the Jagirdar of Sasaram?
    A) Farid
    B) Bahar Khan Lohani
    C) Hasan
    D) Malika
    Answer: C) Hasan

39. From whom did Sher Shah receive the title of Sher Shah?
    A) Humayun
    B) Bahar Khan Lohani, the Afghan Governor of South Bihar
    C) His father Hasan
    D) Islam Shah
    Answer: B) Bahar Khan Lohani, the Afghan Governor of South Bihar

40. How did Sher Shah gain Chunar?
    A) By conquering it in battle
    B) By marrying the widow, Malika
    C) By a peace treaty
    D) By bribing the local ruler
    Answer: B) By marrying the widow, Malika

41. In which battle did Sher Shah defeat Humayun for the first time?
    A) Battle of Kannauj
    B) Battle of Chausa
    C) First Battle of Panipat
    D) Battle of Kalingar
    Answer: B) Battle of Chausa

42. After defeating Humayun at Chausa, what title did Sher Shah adopt?
    A) Sultan-e-Azam
    B) Sher Shah
    C) Alamgir
    D) Zinda Pir
    Answer: B) Sher Shah

43. Which battle is also known as the Battle of Bilgram?
    A) Battle of Chausa
    B) Battle of Kalingar
    C) Battle of Kannauj
    D) Battle of Panipat
    Answer: C) Battle of Kannauj

44. What was Sher Shah’s last campaign against?
    A) Humayun
    B) Sikandar Suri
    C) Kalingar
    D) Sasaram
    Answer: C) Kalingar

45. How did Sher Shah die?
    A) In battle
    B) From illness
    C) From an explosion
    D) Assassinated
    Answer: C) From an explosion

46. When did Sher Shah die?
    A) 1540
    B) 1545
    C) 1555
    D) 1562
    Answer: B) 1545

47. Who succeeded Sher Shah?
    A) Humayun
    B) Sikandar Suri
    C) His second son, Islam Shah
    D) Bahar Khan Lohani
    Answer: C) His second son, Islam Shah

48. Who defeated Sikandar Suri and occupied Delhi in 1555?
    A) Sher Shah
    B) Humayun
    C) Akbar
    D) Ibrahim Lodi
    Answer: B) Humayun

49. How many Sarkars (districts) did Sher Shah divide his empire into?
    A) 12
    B) 24
    C) 47
    D) 60
    Answer: C) 47

50. What was the lowest unit of administration under Sher Shah?
    A) Pargana
    B) Sarkar
    C) Village
    D) Tahsil
    Answer: C) Village

51. Who did Sher Shah make responsible for local crime?
    A) Military commanders
    B) Local village headmen (muqaddams) and zamindars
    C) Special police force
    D) Central government officials
    Answer: B) Local village headmen (muqaddams) and zamindars

52. What was the name of the system Sher Shah adopted to improve the land revenue system?
    A) Dahsala System
    B) Mansabdari system
    C) Zabti-i-Har-Sal
    D) Iqta system
    Answer: C) Zabti-i-Har-Sal

53. Which road did Sher Shah build from Sonargaon to Attock?
    A) Agra-Jodhpur-Chittor Road
    B) Lahore-Multan Road
    C) Grand Trunk Road
    D) Agra-Burhanpur Road
    Answer: C) Grand Trunk Road

54. How many sarais did Sher Shah build, which also worked as dak chaukis?
    A) 47
    B) 1200
    C) 1700
    D) 2000
    Answer: C) 1700

55. At what age did Sher Shah ascend the throne of Delhi?
    A) 40
    B) 50
    C) 67
    D) 75
    Answer: C) 67

56. Who was the historian of Sher Shah?
    A) Abul Fazal
    B) Abbas Khan Sarwani
    C) Faizi
    D) Badauni
    Answer: B) Abbas Khan Sarwani

57. What is the sole surviving building built by Sher Shah in Delhi?
    A) Dinpanah
    B) Sher Mandal
    C) Humayun's Tomb
    D) Old Fort (Purana Qila)
    Answer: D) Old Fort (Purana Qila)

AKBAR (1556-1605 A.D.)

58. Where was Akbar born?
    A) Delhi
    B) Agra
    C) Amarkot in the palace of Virasal
    D) Lahore
    Answer: C) Amarkot in the palace of Virasal

59. In which year was Akbar born?
    A) 1540 A.D.
    B) 1542 A.D.
    C) 1556 A.D.
    D) 1605 A.D.
    Answer: B) 1542 A.D.

60. What was Akbar’s full name?
    A) Zahiruddin Muhammad Akbar
    B) Nasiruddin Muhammad Akbar
    C) Jalaluddin Muhammad Akbar
    D) Muihiuddin Muhammad Akbar
    Answer: C) Jalaluddin Muhammad Akbar

61. At what age was Akbar crowned?
    A) 10 years
    B) 13 years
    C) 15 years
    D) 16 years
    Answer: B) 13 years

62. Where was Akbar crowned?
    A) Delhi
    B) Agra
    C) Kalanaur
    D) Amarkot
    Answer: C) Kalanaur

63. In which battle did Akbar defeat Hemu?
    A) First Battle of Panipat
    B) Second Battle of Panipat
    C) Battle of Haldigati
    D) Battle of Chausa
    Answer: B) Second Battle of Panipat

64. When was the Second Battle of Panipat fought?
    A) 1526 A.D.
    B) 1556 A.D.
    C) 1568 A.D.
    D) 1576 A.D.
    Answer: B) 1556 A.D.

65. Who was Akbar’s regent from 1556 to 1560?
    A) Hemu
    B) Rana Udai Singh
    C) Bairam Khan
    D) Raja Todarmal
    Answer: C) Bairam Khan

66. What religious sect did Bairam Khan belong to?
    A) Sunni
    B) Shia
    C) Sufi
    D) Hindu
    Answer: B) Shia

67. Where was Bairam Khan assassinated?
    A) Mecca
    B) Patan (Gujarat)
    C) Kalanaur
    D) Amarkot
    Answer: B) Patan (Gujarat)

68. What city did Akbar lay the foundation of as his capital?
    A) Agra
    B) Delhi
    C) Fatehpur Sikri
    D) Lahore
    Answer: C) Fatehpur Sikri

69. In which year was Chittor invaded, which was under Rana Udai Singh of Mewar?
    A) 1556
    B) 1568
    C) 1572
    D) 1576
    Answer: B) 1568

70. Which two Rajput warriors fought bravely against Akbar in Chittor?
    A) Rana Pratap and Man Singh
    B) Jaimal and Patta
    C) Rana Udai Singh and Hemu
    D) Prithviraj Chauhan and Jaichandra
    Answer: B) Jaimal and Patta

71. In which battle did Akbar defeat Rana Pratap of Chittor?
    A) Second Battle of Panipat
    B) Battle of Khanwa
    C) Battle of Haldigati
    D) Battle of Chausa
    Answer: C) Battle of Haldigati

72. When was the Battle of Haldigati fought?
    A) 1556
    B) 1568
    C) 1576
    D) 1605
    Answer: C) 1576

73. Which social reforms did Akbar try to implement?
    A) Banning widow marriage
    B) Legalizing sati system
    C) Banning sati system and legalizing widow marriage
    D) Decreasing marriageable age
    Answer: C) Banning sati system and legalizing widow marriage

74. What was the increased marriageable age for girls and boys under Akbar?
    A) Girls 12, Boys 14
    B) Girls 14, Boys 16
    C) Girls 16, Boys 18
    D) Girls 18, Boys 20
    Answer: B) Girls 14, Boys 16

75. What was the 'Ibadat-khana' set up at Sikri for?
    A) Military training
    B) Religious discussion
    C) Revenue collection
    D) Judicial proceedings
    Answer: B) Religious discussion

76. On which day of the week were religious discussions held at Ibadat-khana?
    A) Monday
    B) Wednesday
    C) Thursday
    D) Friday
    Answer: C) Thursday

77. In which year was Ibadat-khana opened to scholars of all faiths?
    A) 1575
    B) 1579
    C) 1580
    D) 1582
    Answer: The text states it was opened "Later", after being set up in 1575. No specific year is given for the opening to all faiths. Therefore, based on the options, the context implies a year after 1575. None of the options precisely match the information. Closest is after 1575, so if a choice needed, it would be one of the later ones.

78. In 1579, who read the Khutba in his own name, like the Prophet and Caliphs?
    A) Faizi
    B) Abul Fazal
    C) Akbar
    D) Bairam Khan
    Answer: C) Akbar

79. Who wrote the Khutba that Akbar read in his own name?
    A) Abul Fazal
    B) Faizi
    C) Tansen
    D) Birbal
    Answer: B) Faizi

80. What was the 'Mahzar' proclaimed in 1579?
    A) A new tax system
    B) A military reform
    C) A proclamation whereby Akbar became Imam-i-Adil, the supreme interpreter of Islamic law
    D) A trade agreement
    Answer: C) A proclamation whereby Akbar became Imam-i-Adil, the supreme interpreter of Islamic law

81. What was Tauhid-i-Ilahi initiated in 1582 later called?
    A) Din-i-Ilahi
    B) Islam-i-Ilahi
    C) Sufism-i-Ilahi
    D) Sikhism-i-Ilahi
    Answer: A) Din-i-Ilahi

82. What was the nature of Din-i-Ilahi?
    A) Orthodox Islamic
    B) Syncretic and Sufistic
    C) Purely Hindu
    D) Atheistic
    Answer: B) Syncretic and Sufistic

83. What were the officials appointed by Akbar after returning from the Gujarat campaign called?
    A) Mansabdars
    B) Jagirdars
    C) Karoris
    D) Zamindars
    Answer: C) Karoris

84. Into how many Subas did Akbar divide the empire in 1580 AD?
    A) 10
    B) 12
    C) 15
    D) 18
    Answer: B) 12

85. When did Akbar die?
    A) 1600 A.D.
    B) 1605 A.D.
    C) 1627 A.D.
    D) 1628 A.D.
    Answer: B) 1605 A.D.

86. What was the cause of Akbar's death?
    A) Battle wounds
    B) Assassination
    C) Dysentery
    D) Old age
    Answer: C) Dysentery

87. Where was Akbar buried?
    A) Delhi
    B) Agra
    C) Sikandara Near Agra
    D) Fatehpur Sikri
    Answer: C) Sikandara Near Agra

88. What was the loan to farmers called under Akbar's reign?
    A) Taqavi
    B) Iqta
    C) Jagir
    D) Peshkash
    Answer: A) Takavi

89. What new form of worship did Akbar start in court?
    A) Idol worship
    B) Prakash Pujas (worshipping light)
    C) Offering namaz five times a day
    D) Animal sacrifice
    Answer: B) Prakash Pujas (worshipping light)

90. With the help of whom did Akbar begin land settlement and launch the Ain-i-Dahsala system?
    A) Abul Fazal
    B) Faizi
    C) Raja Todarmal
    D) Birbal
    Answer: C) Raja Todarmal

91. What were the names of the round and square size silver coins issued by Akbar?
    A) Tanka and Jital
    B) Rupaya and Jalali
    C) Adha and Bikh
    D) Dinar and Dirham
    Answer: B) Rupaya and Jalali

92. What was the court language adopted by Akbar?
    A) Urdu
    B) Hindi
    C) Persian
    D) Turkish
    Answer: C) Persian

93. When did Akbar lay the foundation of Fatehpur Sikri?
    A) 1556-1560
    B) 1568-1570
    C) 1572-1580
    D) 1582-1590
    Answer: C) 1572-1580

94. Which of the following is NOT a main building of Fatehpur Sikri mentioned?
    A) Buland Darwaza
    B) Diwan-i-Am
    C) Moti Masjid
    D) Panch Mahal
    Answer: C) Moti Masjid

95. What musical instrument did Akbar play?
    A) Sitar
    B) Tabla
    C) Nakkara (drum)
    D) Flute
    Answer: C) Nakkara (drum)

96. Who among the following was NOT a court musician of Akbar?
    A) Tansen
    B) Baba Ramdas
    C) Baz Bahadur
    D) Jagannath Pandit
    Answer: D) Jagannath Pandit

97. Which famous painters were in Akbar's court?
    A) Mansur and Abul Hasan
    B) Khwaja Abdus Samad, Daswant, Basawan
    C) Mir Sayyid Ali and Abdus Samad (Humayun's painters)
    D) Ustad Ahmad Lahori and Mirak Mirza Ghiyas
    Answer: B) Khwaja Abdus Samad, Daswant, Basawan

98. What was the Persian translation of Mahabharata called?
    A) Shah Namah
    B) Rehla
    C) Razmnama
    D) Ain-i-Akbari
    Answer: C) Razmnama

99. Who among the following was NOT a contemporary Hindu scholar in Akbar's court?
    A) Ramdas
    B) Suradas
    C) Tulsidas
    D) Jagannath Pandit
    Answer: D) Jagannath Pandit

100. What was Birbal's real name?
    A) Raja Todarmal
    B) Raja Man Singh
    C) Raja Mahesh Das
    D) Raja Bhagwan Das
    Answer: C) Raja Mahesh Das

101. What was Akbar’s land revenue system called?
    A) Iqta system
    B) Mansabdari system
    C) Todar Mal Bandobast or Zabti system
    D) Jaziya system
    Answer: C) Todar Mal Bandobast or Zabti system

102. Who was Akbar’s Revenue Minister?
    A) Abul Fazal
    B) Faizi
    C) Raja Todarmal
    D) Birbal
    Answer: C) Raja Todarmal

103. When did Akbar start the Mansabdari system?
    A) 1562-1564
    B) 1571-1575
    C) 1575-1576
    D) 1580-1582
    Answer: C) 1575-1576

Akbar’s social & Religious work

104. In which year did Akbar end the Slave system?
    A) 1562 AD
    B) 1563 AD
    C) 1564 AD
    D) 1571 AD
    Answer: A) 1562 AD

105. In which year did Akbar end the ‘Haramdal’?
    A) 1562 AD
    B) 1563 AD
    C) 1564 AD
    D) 1571 AD
    Answer: A) 1562 AD

106. In which year did Akbar end the Pilgrim tax?
    A) 1562 AD
    B) 1563 AD
    C) 1564 AD
    D) 1571 AD
    Answer: B) 1563 AD

107. In which year did Akbar end the Jaziya tax?
    A) 1562 AD
    B) 1563 AD
    C) 1564 AD
    D) 1571 AD
    Answer: C) 1564 AD

108. In which year did Akbar establish Fatehpur Sikri and make it his capital?
    A) 1564 AD
    B) 1571 AD
    C) 1575 AD
    D) 1580 AD
    Answer: B) 1571 AD

109. In which year was the ‘Dahsala System’ introduced?
    A) 1571 AD
    B) 1575 AD
    C) 1580 AD
    D) 1582 AD
    Answer: C) 1580 AD

110. In which year was Din-i-Ilahi initiated?
    A) 1562 AD
    B) 1579 AD
    C) 1580 AD
    D) 1582 AD
    Answer: D) 1582 AD

Nine Gems of Akbar

    B) Shivaji
    C) Tansen
    D) Birbal
    Answer: B) Shivaji

    A) Music
    B) Poetry
    C) Revenue administration
    D) Military command
    Answer: C) Revenue administration

    A) Todar Mal
    B) Birbal
    C) Tansen

    Answer: B) Birbal

JAHANGIR (1605-1627 A.D.)

114. Where was Jahangir born?
    A) Agra
    B) Fatehpur Sikri
    C) Lahore
    D) Delhi
    Answer: C) Lahore

115. In which year was Jahangir born?
    A) 1569 A.D.
    B) 1605 A.D.
    C) 1627 A.D.
    D) 1542 A.D.
    Answer: A) 1569 A.D.

116. Besides a Kachchwaha princess, whom else did Jahangir marry?
    A) Mumtaz Mahal
    B) Jodha Bai of Marwar
    C) Hamida Banu Begum
    D) Nur Jahan
    Answer: B) Jodha Bai of Marwar

117. What did Jahangir establish at Agra Fort for the seekers of royal justice?
    A) Diwan-i-Am
    B) Diwan-i-Khas
    C) Zanjir-i-Adal
    D) Ibadat-khana
    Answer: C) Zanjir-i-Adal

118. Which son of Jahangir revolted against him and received patronage of Guru Arjun Dev?
    A) Khurram
    B) Parvez
    C) Khusrau
    D) Shahryar
    Answer: C) Khusrau

119. Who was the fifth Sikh Guru sentenced to death for his blessings to the rebel prince?
    A) Guru Nanak Dev
    B) Guru Angad Dev
    C) Guru Arjun Dev
    D) Guru Govind Singh
    Answer: C) Guru Arjun Dev

120. Which of Jahangir's sons, supported by his father-in-law Asaf Khan, also revolted but later reconciled?
    A) Khusrau
    B) Parvez
    C) Khurram (Shahjahan)
    D) Shahryar
    Answer: C) Khurram (Shahjahan)

121. Which military general of Jahangir revolted and abducted him?
    A) Asaf Khan
    B) Mahabat Khan
    C) Khan-i-Khana
    D) Raja Man Singh
    Answer: B) Mahabat Khan

122. Who saved Jahangir from abduction by Mahabat Khan due to her diplomatic efforts?
    A) Jodha Bai
    B) Mumtaz Mahal
    C) Nur Jahan
    D) Jahanara Begum
    Answer: C) Nur Jahan

123. What were Jahangir's memories, written in Persian, called?
    A) Baburnama
    B) Humayunnama
    C) Tuzuk-i-Jahangiri
    D) Ain-i-Akbari
    Answer: C) Tuzuk-i-Jahangiri

124. Which two English representatives were sent by James-I to Jahangir’s court to get concessions in trade?
    A) Thomas Coryat and Edward Terry
    B) William Finch and Nicholas Downton
    C) Captain Hawkins and Sir Thomas Roe
    D) Ralph Fitch and William Leedes
    Answer: C) Captain Hawkins and Sir Thomas Roe

125. Which garden was laid by Jahangir in Kashmir?
    A) Nishat Bagh
    B) Shalimar Bagh
    C) Pinjore Gardens
    D) Yadavindra Gardens
    Answer: B) Shalimar Bagh

126. Which tomb built by Jahangir was the first building to have Pietra Dura?
    A) Jahangir's tomb in Shahadara
    B) Itmaduddaula’s tomb in Agra
    C) Akbar’s tomb in Sikandara
    D) Humayun's tomb in Delhi
    Answer: B) Itmaduddaula’s tomb in Agra

127. Which Mughal art reached its climax during Jahangir's reign?
    A) Architecture
    B) Calligraphy
    C) Painting
    D) Music
    Answer: C) Painting

128. What type of paintings became prominent during Jahangir's reign?
    A) Fresco paintings
    B) Portrait painting and paintings of animals & birds
    C) Miniature paintings of historical events
    D) Abstract art
    Answer: B) Portrait painting and paintings of animals & birds

129. Who was an expert in portrait painting and paintings of animals & birds during Jahangir's reign?
    A) Daswant
    B) Basawan
    C) Mansur
    D) Khwaja Abdus Samad
    Answer: C) Mansur

130. Which systems did Jahangir introduce in the Mansabdari system?
    A) Zabti and Dahsala
    B) Iqta and Jagir
    C) Du-Aspa and Sih-Aspa
    D) Qurbani and Jaziya
    Answer: C) Du-Aspa and Sih-Aspa

131. When did Jahangir meet Nur Jahan?
    A) On Eid
    B) On Nauroz (new year's day)
    C) During Diwali
    D) At a royal wedding
    Answer: B) On Nauroz (new year's day)

132. Where did Jahangir meet Nur Jahan?
    A) Shalimar Bagh
    B) Mina Bazar of Agra
    C) Lahore Fort
    D) Delhi Darbar
    Answer: B) Mina Bazar of Agra

133. When did Jahangir marry Nur Jahan?
    A) 1605
    B) 1611
    C) 1613
    D) 1620
    Answer: B) 1611

134. What title was given to Nur Jahan in 1613?
    A) Jodha Bai
    B) Mumtaz Mahal
    C) Padshah Begum
    D) Begum Sahiba
    Answer: C) Padshah Begum

135. Who ordered Khusrau to be blinded and later killed in Burhanpur in 1620?
    A) Jahangir
    B) Mahabat Khan
    C) Khurram
    D) Nur Jahan
    Answer: C) Khurram

136. For how long did John Hawkins reside at Agra?
    A) One year
    B) Two years
    C) Three years
    D) Four years
    Answer: B) Two years (1609-1611)

137. What mansab was given to John Hawkins?
    A) 100
    B) 200
    C) 400
    D) 500
    Answer: C) 400

138. Who was the ambassador of James-I from 1615-1618?
    A) Captain Hawkins
    B) John Mildenhall
    C) Sir Thomas Roe
    D) William Hawkins
    Answer: C) Sir Thomas Roe

139. Where was Jahangir buried?
    A) Agra
    B) Delhi
    C) Sikandara
    D) Shahadara near Lahore
    Answer: D) Shahadara near Lahore

    D) Manucci
    Answer: B) Pietro Valle

SHAHJAHAN (1628-1657 A.D.)

141. Where was Shahjahan born?
    A) Agra
    B) Delhi
    C) Lahore
    D) Burhanpur
    Answer: C) Lahore

142. In which year was Shahjahan born?
    A) 1592 A.D.
    B) 1605 A.D.
    C) 1628 A.D.
    D) 1631 A.D.
    Answer: A) 1592 A.D.

143. What was Shahjahan’s childhood name?
    A) Khusrau
    B) Khurram
    C) Dara Shikoh
    D) Aurangzeb
    Answer: B) Khurram

144. To whom was Shahjahan born?
    A) Nur Jahan
    B) Jodha Bai
    C) Jagat Gosain, daughter of Mota Raja Udai Singh of Jodhpur
    D) Mumtaz Mahal
    Answer: C) Jagat Gosain, daughter of Mota Raja Udai Singh of Jodhpur

145. In which year did Shahjahan marry Arzumand Banu Begum?
    A) 1605
    B) 1611
    C) 1612
    D) 1631
    Answer: C) 1612

146. By what name did Arzumand Banu Begum become famous?
    A) Nur Jahan
    B) Jahanara Begum
    C) Mumtaz Mahal
    D) Roshan Ara Begum
    Answer: C) Mumtaz Mahal

147. When did Mumtaz Mahal die?
    A) 1612
    B) 1628
    C) 1631
    D) 1654
    Answer: C) 1631

148. Where did Mumtaz Mahal die during childbirth?
    A) Agra
    B) Delhi
    C) Burhanpur
    D) Lahore
    Answer: C) Burhanpur

149. Where was Mumtaz Mahal first buried?
    A) Agra
    B) Burhanpur
    C) Delhi
    D) Lahore
    Answer: B) Burhanpur

150. Where was Mumtaz Mahal later transferred and buried?
    A) Delhi (Taj Mahal)
    B) Agra (Taj Mahal)
    C) Lahore (Taj Mahal)
    D) Aurangabad
    Answer: B) Agra (Taj Mahal)

151. Which of the following was NOT a child of Shah Jahan from Mumtaz Begum?
    A) Jahanara Begum
    B) Dara Shikoh
    C) Jahangir
    D) Aurangzeb
    Answer: C) Jahangir

152. Where was Aurangzeb born?
    A) Agra
    B) Delhi
    C) Lahore
    D) Daulatabad
    Answer: D) Daulatabad

153. What Mansabdari method did Shah Jahan introduce regarding horsemen?
    A) Du-Aspa and Sih-Aspa
    B) 1/3, 1/4, 1/5 method of his sawar rank
    C) Zabti-i-Har-Sal
    D) Dahsala System
    Answer: B) 1/3, 1/4, 1/5 method of his sawar rank

154. When was the Taj Mahal built?
    A) 1612-1631
    B) 1628-1657
    C) 1632-1654
    D) 1631-1658
    Answer: C) 1632-1654

155. What material was primarily used to build the Taj Mahal?
    A) Red sandstone
    B) White marbles
    C) Granite
    D) Yellow sandstone
    Answer: B) White marbles

156. Which mosque did Shah Jahan build in Agra Fort?
    A) Jama Masjid
    B) Moti Masjid (Pearl Mosque)
    C) Wazir Khan Mosque
    D) Badshahi Mosque
    Answer: B) Moti Masjid (Pearl Mosque)

157. Which city did Shah Jahan lay the foundation of, known as the 7th city of Delhi?
    A) Fatehpur Sikri
    B) Tughlaqabad
    C) Shahjahanabad
    D) Dinpanah
    Answer: C) Shahjahanabad

158. Which buildings constructed by Shah Jahan were made of red sandstone?
    A) Taj Mahal and Moti Masjid
    B) Red Fort of Delhi and Jama Masjid
    C) Lahore Fort and Shalimar Bagh
    D) Itmaduddaula’s tomb and Humayun’s tomb
    Answer: B) Red Fort of Delhi and Jama Masjid

159. Who was the poet in Shah Jahan's court?
    A) Tansen
    B) Jagannath Pandit
    C) Faizi
    D) Amir Khusro
    Answer: B) Jagannath Pandit

    A) Takht-i-Jahangiri
    B) Peacock throne
    C) Black stone throne
    D) Golden throne
    Answer: B) Peacock throne

161. When was the Peacock throne constructed?
    A) 1628-1635
    B) 1632-1654
    C) 1605-1627
    D) 1658-1707
    Answer: A) 1628-1635

162. Against whom did Shah Jahan attack Hooghly and capture it in 1632?
    A) Dutch
    B) English
    C) Portuguese
    D) French
    Answer: C) Portuguese

163. Which of the following foreign travelers visited Shah Jahan's court?
    A) Captain Hawkins and Sir Thomas Roe
    B) Nicolo Conti and Abdur Razzaq
    C) Bernier (France), Travernier (France) and Manucci (Italy)
    D) Pietro Valle and John Hawkins
    Answer: C) Bernier (France), Travernier (France) and Manucci (Italy)

164. What was Shah Jahan's period considered the climax of?
    A) Mughal painting
    B) Mughal music
    C) Mughal literature
    D) Mughal architecture
    Answer: D) Mughal architecture

AURANGZEB (1658-1707 A.D.)

165. What was Aurangzeb’s full name?
    A) Zahiruddin Muhammad Aurangzeb
    B) Nasiruddin Muhammad Aurangzeb
    C) Jalaluddin Muhammad Aurangzeb
    D) Muihiuddin Muhammad Aurangzeb
    Answer: D) Muihiuddin Muhammad Aurangzeb

166. Aurangzeb was the son of which Mughal empress?
    A) Jodha Bai
    B) Nur Jahan
    C) Mumtaz Mahal
    D) Hamida Banu Begum
    Answer: C) Mumtaz Mahal

167. What did Aurangzeb have to fight to gain the throne?
    A) Foreign invasions
    B) War of succession
    C) Religious disputes
    D) Economic crisis
    Answer: B) War of succession

168. What title did Aurangzeb take in 1659?
    A) Padshah Begum
    B) Alamgir
    C) Zinda Pir
    D) Sultan-e-Azam
    Answer: B) Alamgir

169. What was Aurangzeb called due to his ascetic lifestyle?
    A) Living King
    B) Living Saint (Zinda Pir)
    C) Great Ruler
    D) Just Emperor
    Answer: B) Living Saint (Zinda Pir)

170. Which inscription did Aurangzeb forbid on the coins?
    A) Emperor's name
    B) Kalma
    C) Year of minting
    D) Value of coin
    Answer: B) Kalma

171. Which festival did Aurangzeb end the celebration of?
    A) Eid
    B) Diwali
    C) Navroz
    D) Holi
    Answer: C) Navroz festival

172. What were 'Mutasib' appointed for under Aurangzeb?
    A) Revenue collection
    B) Military command
    C) Regulation of moral conduct
    D) Judicial proceedings
    Answer: C) Regulation of moral conduct

173. Which practices did Aurangzeb end?
    A) Jharokha darshan, use of almanacs and weighing of the emperor
    B) Use of elephants in war
    C) Building new monuments
    D) Writing memoirs
    Answer: A) Jharokha darshan, use of almanacs and weighing of the emperor

174. Which tax was re-introduced by Aurangzeb?
    A) Pilgrim tax
    B) Jaziya
    C) Ghari
    D) Chari
    Answer: B) Jaziya

175. Which mosque did Aurangzeb build in Red Fort of Delhi?
    A) Jama Masjid
    B) Moti Masjid (Pearl Mosque)
    C) Badshahi Mosque
    D) Wazir Khan Mosque
    Answer: B) Moti Masjid (Pearl Mosque)

176. During whose reign was the proportion of Hindu mansabdars the highest?
    A) Akbar
    B) Jahangir
    C) Shahjahan
    D) Aurangzeb
    Answer: D) Aurangzeb

177. Which Sikh Guru did Aurangzeb order the arrest and execution of in AD 1675?
    A) Guru Nanak Dev
    B) Guru Arjun Dev
    C) Guru Teg Bahadur
    D) Guru Govind Singh
    Answer: C) Guru Teg Bahadur

178. Who wrote letters to Aurangzeb, whose collection is called Zafarnama?
    A) Guru Teg Bahadur
    B) Guru Govind Singh
    C) Shivaji
    D) Shambhaji
    Answer: B) Guru Govind Singh

179. Where was Shambhaji (son of Shivaji) captured and executed?
    A) Bijapur
    B) Golconda
    C) Sanghameshwar
    D) Raigarh
    Answer: C) Sanghameshwar

180. In which years were Bijapur and Golconda annexed by Aurangzeb?
    A) Bijapur (1686) and Golconda (1687)
    B) Bijapur (1687) and Golconda (1686)
    C) Bijapur (1680) and Golconda (1681)
    D) Bijapur (1690) and Golconda (1691)
    Answer: A) Bijapur (1686) and Golconda (1687)

181. Where is Aurangzeb’s tomb located?
    A) Delhi
    B) Agra
    C) Lahore
    D) Aurangabad, Maharashtra
    Answer: D) Aurangabad, Maharashtra

182. What musical instrument was Aurangzeb an expert in?
    A) Tabla
    B) Sitar
    C) Vina
    D) Flute
    Answer: C) Vina

183. What did Aurangzeb ban in court, but allowed Royal Band (Naubat)?
    A) Painting
    B) Singing
    C) Poetry recitation
    D) Dancing
    Answer: B) Singing

184. Which tax did Aurangzeb restart?
    A) Jaziya
    B) Pilgrim tax
    C) Ghari
    D) Chari
    Answer: B) Pilgrim tax

185. What cultivation did Aurangzeb disallow?
    A) Cotton
    B) Indigo
    C) Hemp (Bhang)
    D) Opium
    Answer: C) Hemp (Bhang)

186. In which year did Jats under Gokla revolt?
    A) 1667
    B) 1669
    C) 1672
    D) 1685
    Answer: B) 1669

187. In which year did Satnamis, nick-named Mundiya Sadhus, revolt?
    A) 1667
    B) 1669
    C) 1672
    D) 1685
    Answer: C) 1672

188. Who founded the Satnamis near Narnaul?
    A) Gokla
    B) Raja Ram
    C) Birbhan
    D) Akmal Khan
    Answer: C) Birbhan

189. In which year did the second Jat rebellion occur under Raja Ram and Churaman?
    A) 1669
    B) 1672
    C) 1685
    D) 1689
    Answer: C) 1685

190. In which year did the Yusufzai tribe of Afghans, under the Raushanai movement led by Bhagu, rebel?
    A) 1667
    B) 1669
    C) 1672
    D) 1685
    Answer: A) 1667

191. In which year did the second Afghan rebellion, led by Akmal Khan of the Afridi tribe, occur?
    A) 1667
    B) 1669
    C) 1672
    D) 1685
    Answer: C) 1672

MUGHAL ADMINISTRATION (Political system)

192. What was the department associated with the 'Sadra' post in Mughal administration?
    A) Finance
    B) Civil Services on Military line
    C) Religious interest
    D) Judiciary department
    Answer: C) Religious interest

193. What was the 'Dewan' department responsible for in Mughal administration?
    A) Religious interest
    B) Finance
    C) Management of Royal farry
    D) Forest
    Answer: B) Finance

194. What was the 'Mirbakshi' responsible for in Mughal administration?
    A) Religious interest
    B) Finance
    C) Civil Services on Military line
    D) Management of Royal farry
    Answer: C) Civil Services on Military line

195. What was the 'Mir Bahar' responsible for in Mughal administration?
    A) Religious interest
    B) Finance
    C) Admiral
    D) Forest
    Answer: C) Admiral

196. What was the 'Khansama' responsible for in Mughal administration?
    A) Management of Royal farry
    B) Forest
    C) Judiciary department
    D) Festival
    Answer: A) Management of Royal farry

197. What was the 'Mir Barr' responsible for in Mughal administration?
    A) Management of Royal farry
    B) Forest
    C) Judiciary department
    D) Festival
    Answer: B) Forest

198. What was the 'Kaji' responsible for in Mughal administration?
    A) Religious interest
    B) Finance
    C) Judiciary department
    D) Civil Services on Military line
    Answer: C) Judiciary department

199. What was the 'Mir Tajak' responsible for in Mughal administration?
    A) Religious interest
    B) Finance
    C) Judiciary department
    D) Festival
    Answer: D) Festival

DECLINE OF MUGHAL EMPIRE - List of Later Mughals

200. Who was known as Shah-i-Bekhabar?
    A) Bahadur Shah I
    B) Jahandar Shah
    C) Farrukh Siyar
    D) Mohammad Shah
    Answer: A) Bahadur Shah I

201. Why was Bahadur Shah I called Shah-i-Bekhabar?
    A) He was a great warrior.
    B) He pursued a pacifist policy.
    C) He was a pleasure-loving ruler.
    D) He abolished Jaziya.
    Answer: B) He pursued a pacifist policy.

202. When was the reign of Bahadur Shah I?
    A) 1707-1712
    B) 1712-1713
    C) 1713-1719
    D) 1719-1748
    Answer: A) 1707-1712

203. Who ascended the throne with the aid of Zulfikar Khan?
    A) Bahadur Shah I
    B) Jahandar Shah
    C) Farrukh Siyar
    D) Mohammad Shah
    Answer: B) Jahandar Shah

204. Who was the first puppet Mughal emperor?
    A) Bahadur Shah I
    B) Jahandar Shah
    C) Farrukh Siyar
    D) Mohammad Shah
    Answer: B) Jahandar Shah

205. Which Later Mughal emperor abolished Jaziya?
    A) Bahadur Shah I
    B) Jahandar Shah
    C) Farrukh Siyar
    D) Mohammad Shah
    Answer: B) Jahandar Shah

206. When was the reign of Jahandar Shah?
    A) 1707-1712
    B) 1712-1713
    C) 1713-1719
    D) 1719-1748
    Answer: B) 1712-1713

207. Who succeeded to the throne with the help of Sayyid brothers (Abdullah Khan and Hussain Ali)?
    A) Jahandar Shah
    B) Farrukh Siyar
    C) Mohammad Shah
    D) Ahmad Shah
    Answer: B) Farrukh Siyar

208. In 1719, with whose help did Sayyid brothers kill Farrukh Siyar?
    A) Nadir Shah
    B) Ahmad Shah Abdali
    C) Maratha Peshwa, Balaji Vishwanath
    D) Zulfikar Khan
    Answer: C) Maratha Peshwa, Balaji Vishwanath

209. When was the reign of Farrukh Siyar?
    A) 1712-1713
    B) 1713-1719
    C) 1719-1748
    D) 1748-1754
    Answer: B) 1713-1719

210. Who was called Mohammad Shah ‘Rangila’?
    A) Farrukh Siyar
    B) Mohammad Shah
    C) Ahmad Shah
    D) Alamgir II
    Answer: B) Mohammad Shah

211. In which battle did Nadir Shah defeat Mohammad Shah?
    A) Battle of Plassey
    B) Battle of Buxar
    C) Battle of Karnal
    D) Third Battle of Panipat
    Answer: C) Battle of Karnal

212. When was the Battle of Karnal fought?
    A) 1719
    B) 1739
    C) 1748
    D) 1754
    Answer: B) 1739

213. What valuable items did Nadir Shah take away after defeating Mohammad Shah?
    A) Kohinoor diamond and Peacock throne
    B) Mughal treasury and royal elephants
    C) Gold and silver coins
    D) All imperial jewels
    Answer: A) Peacock throne and the kohinoor diomand

214. When was the reign of Mohammad Shah?
    A) 1713-1719
    B) 1719-1748
    C) 1748-1754
    D) 1754-1759
    Answer: B) 1719-1748

215. During whose reign did Ahmad Shah Abdali invade India twice (1749 and 1752)?
    A) Mohammad Shah
    B) Ahmad Shah
    C) Alamgir II
    D) Shah Alam II
    Answer: B) Ahmad Shah

216. When was the reign of Ahmad Shah?
    A) 1719-1748
    B) 1748-1754
    C) 1754-1759
    D) 1759-1806
    Answer: B) 1748-1754

217. After the dethronement of Ahmad Shah, who was placed on the throne as Alamgir II?
    A) A grandson of Jahandar Shah, Azizuddin
    B) A son of Mohammad Shah
    C) A son of Farrukh Siyar
    D) A son of Aurangzeb
    Answer: A) Azizuddin a grandson of Jahandar Shah

218. When was the reign of Alamgir II?
    A) 1748-1754
    B) 1754-1759
    C) 1759-1806
    D) 1806-1837
    Answer: B) 1754-1759

219. During whose reign was the Battle of Buxar (1764) held?
    A) Alamgir II
    B) Shah Alam II
    C) Akbar II
    D) Bahadur Shah II
    Answer: B) Shah Alam II

220. When was the reign of Shah Alam II?
    A) 1754-1759
    B) 1759-1806
    C) 1806-1837
    D) 1837-1857
    Answer: B) 1759-1806

221. Who gave the title of Raja to Ram Mohan Roy?
    A) Shah Alam II
    B) Akbar II
    C) Bahadur Shah II
    D) Mohammad Shah
    Answer: B) Akbar II

222. When was the reign of Akbar II?
    A) 1759-1806
    B) 1806-1837
    C) 1837-1857
    D) 1707-1712
    Answer: B) 1806-1837

223. During which revolt was Bahadur Shah II proclaimed the emperor by the rebels?
    A) Revolt of 1857
    B) Indigo Revolt
    C) Sanyasi Rebellion
    D) Munda Rebellion
    Answer: A) Revolt of 1857

224. When was the reign of Bahadur Shah II?
    A) 1806-1837
    B) 1837-1857
    C) 1759-1806
    D) 1707-1712
    Answer: B) 1837-1857


RELIGIOUS MOVEMENTS OF MEDIEVAL INDIA

1.  When did the Bhakti Movement begin in South India?
    A) 6th Century
    B) 7th Century
    C) 8th Century
    D) 9th Century
    Answer: B) 7th Century

2.  What were the Vaishnavite saints of the Bhakti cult in South India called?
    A) Naynars
    B) Alvars
    C) Lingayats
    D) Varkaris
    Answer: B) Alvars

3.  What were the Shaivite saints of the Bhakti cult in South India called?
    A) Alvars
    B) Naynars
    C) Lingayats
    D) Varkaris
    Answer: B) Naynars

4.  How many Alvar Vaishnavite saints were there?
    A) 10
    B) 12
    C) 63
    D) 72
    Answer: B) 12

5.  How many Naynar Saivite saints were there?
    A) 12
    B) 24
    C) 63
    D) 108
    Answer: C) 63

6.  The writings of the Alvars and Naynars were collected in which compilation?
    A) Bhagavad Gita
    B) Adi Granth
    C) Tirumurai
    D) Dasabodha
    Answer: C) Tirumurai

7.  What has the 'Tirumurai' been called?
    A) The Kannada Veda
    B) The Telugu Veda
    C) The Tamil Veda
    D) The Malayalam Veda
    Answer: C) The Tamil Veda

8.  Who composed 'Periya Puranam'?
    A) Ramananda
    B) Shekkilar
    C) Kulottunga I
    D) Tulsidas
    Answer: B) Shekkilar

9.  During the reign of which Chola king was 'Periya Puranam' composed?
    A) Rajaraja Chola I
    B) Rajendra Chola I
    C) Kulottunga I
    D) Vikramaditya VI
    Answer: C) Kulottunga I

10. Who was the first great Bhakti saint of North India?
    A) Kabir
    B) Guru Nanak
    C) Ramananda
    D) Chaitanya
    Answer: C) Ramananda

11. Which of the following was NOT a disciple of Ramananda?
    A) Raidas
    B) Kabir
    C) Dhanna
    D) Tulsidas
    Answer: D) Tulsidas

12. What was Kabir's approximate lifespan?
    A) 1270-1350
    B) 1440-1518
    C) 1469-1539
    D) 1486-1533
    Answer: B) 1440-1518

13. To which profession did Kabir belong?
    A) Tanner
    B) Tailor

    D) Peasant
    Answer: C) Weavers

    C) Rajasthan

    Answer: C) Adi Granth

16. What was Raidas's profession?

    B) Tanner

SOME SECTS DURING BHAKTI MOVEMENT

55. Which garden was built by Babur in Agra?
    A) Shalimar Bagh
    B) Hast Bahist Rambagh
    C) Nishat Bagh
    D) Hayat Bagh
    Answer: B) Hast Bahist Rambagh

56. Which garden was built by Jahangir in Srinagar?
    A) Hast Bahist Rambagh
    B) Shalimar Bagh (Srinagar)
    C) Nishat Bagh
    D) Shailimar Bagh (Lahore)
    Answer: B) Shalimar Bagh (Srinagar)

57. Who built Nishat Bagh in Srinagar?
    A) Jahangir
    B) Asaf Khan
    C) Shahjahan
    D) Dara Shikoh
    Answer: B) Asaf Khan

58. Which garden was built by Shah Jahan in Lahore?
    A) Hast Bahist Rambagh
    B) Shalimar Bagh (Srinagar)
    C) Nishat Bagh
    D) Shailimar Bagh (Lahore)
    Answer: D) Shailimar Bagh (Lahore)

59. Which garden was built by Shah Jahan in Delhi?
    A) Hast Bahist Rambagh
    B) Shalimar Bagh (Srinagar)
    C) Hayat Bagh
    D) Wazir Bagh
    Answer: C) Hayat Bagh

60. Which garden was built by Shah Jahan in Delhi, besides Hayat Bagh?
    A) Shailimar Bagh (Lahore)
    B) Shalimar Bagh (Delhi)
    C) Wazir Bagh
    D) Char Burji Bagh
    Answer: B) Shalimar Bagh (Delhi)

61. Who built Wazir Bagh in Kashmir?
    A) Shahjahan
    B) Dara Shikoh
    C) Zebunnisa
    D) Asaf Khan
    Answer: B) Dara Shikoh

62. Who built Char Burji Bagh near Lahore?
    A) Shahjahan
    B) Dara Shikoh
    C) Zebunnisa
    D) Jahangir
    Answer: C) Zebunnisa

63. Who was the earliest Sufi saint to have settled in India?
    A) Muinuddin Chishti
    B) Hamiduddin Nagauri
    C) Al Hujuiri
    D) Farid Ganj-i-Shakar
    Answer: C) Al Hujuiri

64. Who wrote ‘Kashful Mahjub’?
    A) Muinuddin Chishti
    B) Al Hujuiri
    C) Bahauddin Zakaria
    D) Shaikh Nizamuddin Auliya
    Answer: B) Al Hujuiri

65. Which two Sufi orders were the most prominent in India?
    A) Kadari and Satari
    B) Firdoshi and Naqshabandi
    C) Chishti and Suhrawardi
    D) Sabiria and Chishtia
    Answer: C) Chishti and Suhrawardi

66. Who founded the Chishti order in India?
    A) Muinuddin Chishti
    B) Qutubuddin Bakhtiyar Kaki
    C) Farid Ganj-i-Shakar
    D) Shaikh Nizamuddin Auliya
    Answer: A) Muinuddin Chishti

67. When did Muinuddin Chishti arrive in India?
    A) Around 1100 A.D.
    B) Around 1192 A.D.
    C) Around 1270 A.D.
    D) Around 1350 A.D.
    Answer: B) Around 1192 A.D.

68. Where did Muinuddin Chishti establish his center?
    A) Delhi
    B) Ajmer
    C) Multan
    D) Ajodhan
    Answer: B) Ajmer

69. Who was Muinuddin Chishti’s disciple?
    A) Qutubuddin Bakhtiyar Kaki
    B) Hamiduddin Nagauri
    C) Farid Ganj-i-Shakar
    D) Shaikh Nizamuddin Auliya
    Answer: B) Hamiduddin Nagauri

70. Qutubminar is named after which Chishti saint?
    A) Muinuddin Chishti
    B) Qutubuddin Bakhtiyar Kaki
    C) Farid Ganj-i-Shakar
    D) Shaikh Nizamuddin Auliya
    Answer: B) Qutubuddin Bakhtiyar Kaki

    A) Muinuddin Chishti
    B) Hamiduddin Nagauri
    C) Farid Ganj-i-Shakar
    D) Shaikh Nizamuddin Auliya
    Answer: C) Farid Ganj-i-Shakar
